| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `auth.agegate.layout.dateEntry` | `{"control":"C-12 text field (fill color.global.control, size.global.fieldHeight, radius.global.field, text role placeholder) with a numeric keyboard","format":"the device locale's numeric short-date order (DateFormatter.dateFormat(fromTemplate: \"MMddyyyy\", options: 0, locale: .current) / DateTimeFormatterBuilder.getLocalizedDateTimePattern(FormatStyle.SHORT, null, IsoChronology.INSTANCE, locale)), shown as the placeholder","default":"empty: no pre-filled date, no default year, no age options","threshold":"the minimum age is never shown before the person submits"}` | FTC COPPA FAQ | birthday entry (neutral age screen) | docs/PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS.md §9 (FTC COPPA FAQ, https://www.ftc.gov/business-guidance/resources/complying-coppa-frequently-asked-questions: a neutral age screen 'allows a user freely to enter month and year of birth'); field: design/components.md C-12 | VERIFIED | Retro confirms age with a sentence in the landing fine print ('I confirm that I am %1$d or older', LAND) instead of a date entry; the neutral age screen is kept for COPPA (MERGE RA-4). |
| `auth.agegate.layout.optIn` | `{"control":"C-14 grouped section with a single row: title (text role body) + subtitle (text role meta, color.global.textSecondary) + trailing native switch (tint color.global.selection / color.global.system.primary)","default":"off","inset":"size.global.screenMargin"}` | Retro | opt-in row 'Share My Birthday' under the birthday field | design/components.md C-14 (VID-SET grouped rows; APK-X pik 12 dp shapes); references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt settings_title_lock_friend_requests / settings_description_lock_friend_requests (Title-Case row + one-sentence description) | SOURCED | composition of Retro components C-14 (grouped row) + native switch (PLATFORM) in the order of the old opt-in check row · Retro has no birthday opt-in; its settings rows are the closest component. |

## `camera.capture`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |

| `camera.capture.duration.videoMax` | `10000 ms` | Locket | maximum length of a hold-to-record video (premium); the recording outline fills over this time | references/auto/locket/lazyweb/ (LZ 2026-08 paywall) perk 'Longer videos / Record up to 10 seconds' (design/research/locket.md §10) | SOURCED | verbatim perk text · Mechanics (Locket). Unchanged by the redesign. |
| `camera.capture.layout.controlRow` | `{"centre":"the shutter, horizontally centred","sideControls":"two circles of size.global.circleButtonLarge (64): library (leading), flip (trailing)","sideCentre":"each side circle is centred in the free space between the screen edge and the shutter's outer edge","vertical":"the row is vertically centred between the viewfinder bottom and the tab bar top (daily.capture: the safe-area bottom)","minimumGap":"size.global.screenMargin above and below the shutter"}` | Retro | control row under the viewfinder: shutter flanked by two 64-pt circles (Retro's central control flanked by 64-pt circles, C-35) | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320×2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): left circle x118–310, y2192–2384 (64 × 64 pt), centre x 71.3 pt; right circle x1009–1201, centre 71.7 pt from the right edge; dial 150 pt Ø centred → (440 ÷ 2 − 75) ÷ 2 = 72.5. references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS JPEG 1260×2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): circle centres 67.7 / 68.3 pt from the edges on a 420-pt screen → (420 ÷ 2 − 75) ÷ 2 = 67.5 | SOURCED | edge scans of the glass circles on both RAW captures; the side circles sit centred between the screen edge and the central control's edge (±1 pt on both devices) · ±1 pt · Rule measured on Retro's Rewind (design/components.md C-35) and applied with the shutter as the central control. Side circles on the black page use the 'circle on media' fill color.global.controlOnDeep (C-11): glass is only drawn over photos (layout.global.materials). |
| `camera.capture.layout.viewfinder` | `{"aspect":0.75,"width":"screen width − 2 × size.global.screenMargin","corners":"radius.global.viewfinder","top":"size.global.titleToContent below the header title baseline (C-01)","fill":"aspect-fill live preview; what the frame shows is what is captured","shrink":"if the control row would get less than size.global.screenMargin above and below the shutter, the viewfinder height shrinks (3:4 kept, centred horizontally)"}` | Retro | live viewfinder: a Retro photo container (3:4, 16-pt margins, 18-pt continuous corners) under the root header | Retro photo containers: references/auto/retro/lazyweb/retro-lazyweb-20251020-week43-dark.png (strip at the 16 margin, 3:4 tiles, outer corners 18), references/auto/retro/store/retro-store-01.png (friend card 18 corners) — design/research/retro-visual.md §3.4, §3.5, §3.8; header C-01 (design/components.md); mechanics order: Locket camera (viewfinder → control row), references/auto/locket/store/locket-store-07.png (AS-7, Locket App Store 2026, 2.036 px/pt) — design/research/locket.md §5 'Measured values' | SOURCED | composition of Retro components C-01 (root header), C-17/C-18 photo container (radius.global.viewfinder), C-04 (tab bar) in Locket's order (viewfinder above the shutter row) · 3:4 is Retro's single-photo container shape (strip tiles 84 × 112, album covers 72 × 97 — retro-visual §3.8) and the camera's native 4:3 photo output (PLATFORM), so the frame is captured as shown. Replaces Yope's full-width 1 : 1.38 viewfinder (MERGE_DECISIONS RC-2). A full-bleed preview (C-35) was rejected: a 3:4 photo filling a 393 × 852 screen shows only 61 % of the captured width (RC-2). |

## `camera.fromwidget`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `camera.fromwidget.layout.target` | `{"link":"app://camera?group=<group_id>","ios":"widgetURL on the whole widget","android":"PendingIntent ACTION_VIEW on the widget root","opens":"camera.capture, group preselected in camera.sendto"}` | Locket | what a widget tap does | references/auto/locket/store/locket-store-07.png (deskewed 2.0°; 2.036 px/pt; App Store listing v2.68.0) headline 'Tap the widget to open the camera'; references/auto/locket/play/locket-play-05.png; Locket Android APK 1.243.0 (APKMirror, uploaded 2026-10-09; design/research/locket.md §2) Hermes strings 'logOpenedFromWidget', 'Failed to parse widget launch URL'; https://developer.apple.com/documentation/widgetkit/adding-interactivity-to-widgets-and-live-activities ('a button should do more than open the app' → widgetURL, not a Button) | SOURCED | store copy + bundle strings; native API · Mechanics (Locket: 'Tap the widget to open the camera'). Unchanged by the redesign; Retro's own widget tap reloads (do-not-copy for us: our widget opens the camera). |

## `chat.thread`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `chat.thread.count.emojiOnlyMax` | `3` | Apple Messages + WhatsApp | a text message of only 1–3 emoji renders large (fontSize.global.headerTitle, 40 pt glyphs); 4 or more render as normal text | references/auto/imessage/web/slashgear-2023-09-imessage-big-emoji.png ('up to three different ones'); references/auto/whatsapp/web/xataka-whatsapp-emojis-gigantes.png ('A partir de cuatro emojis, el tamaño es igual') | SOURCED | rule stated by two references (Gap fill G-2) · rule · Size changed in the redesign: the emoji take Retro's largest type size (fontSize.global.headerTitle) instead of Yope's 47.5-pt glyph (MERGE RS-4). |
| `chat.thread.dimension.itemGap` | `8 pt` | Retro | gap between stacked items inside one run (text block, photo card, voice capsule, post attachment, reaction chip) | references/auto/retro/web/retro.app-42b38811bebcd07d.css (.immersiveViewer_mediaFooterText__0UFYr{…gap:.5rem}: the stacked lines of Retro's viewer footer) | SOURCED | CSS constant .5rem = 8 CSS px = 8 pt · exact constant · Composition: Retro's gap between stacked items of one media block, applied to the items of one message run (MERGE RS-3). |
| `chat.thread.dimension.rowAvatarToText` | `16.7 pt` | Retro | message row (C-16 comment row): avatar right edge → name / text ink | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2023-07-retro-2b.jpg (viewer comment row 'elton 2h / whoa!': avatar x 1356–1423, text ink from x 1460) | SOURCED | run edges, 37 px ÷ 2.209 px/pt — TC-2B phone 2 at 2.209 px/pt (the full-width photo spans x 1320–2188 = 868 px = 393 pt; the 16-pt field margins and the 8.6-pt home-indicator offset agree) · ±0.5 pt (JPEG render 2023) · Not in design/components.md C-16 (it names sizes only). The viewer comments (photo.viewer) and the chat thread use the same row. Avatar top aligns with the name line's ascender (avatar y 1704 vs ink 1701). |
| `chat.thread.dimension.rowLinePitch` | `19 pt` | Retro | message row: baseline of the name / time line → baseline of the first text line (and text line → text line) | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2023-07-retro-2b.jpg ('elton 2h' baseline y 1724 → 'whoa!' baseline y 1766) | SOURCED | 42 px ÷ 2.209 px/pt — TC-2B phone 2 at 2.209 px/pt (the full-width photo spans x 1320–2188 = 868 px = 393 pt; the 16-pt field margins and the 8.6-pt home-indicator offset agree) · ±0.5 pt · Equals the 15-pt body role's line pitch on iOS; multi-line message text keeps this pitch. |
| `chat.thread.duration.entry` | `250 ms` | Retro | a new message row appearing at the bottom of the thread (fade in) | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt (m6a.java:1509 nrh.X(250, 0, qh4.d, 2) "entry animation" — message thread); design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.7 | SOURCED | Compose tween constant read from the package · exact constant · Reduce Motion: the row appears without a fade (layout.global.reduceMotion keeps fades; this one is a cross-fade of the same duration, allowed). |
| `chat.thread.easing.entry` | `linear` | Retro | curve of the message entry animation | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt (m6a.java:1509 tween easing qh4.d); design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.7 ('tween 250 ms Linear — "entry animation" (message thread)') | SOURCED | Compose easing constant identified as LinearEasing in the package research · exact constant |
| `chat.thread.layout.failedMessage` | `{"indicator":"one line under the failed message: chat.thread.sendFailed, text role caption, color.global.destructive; no glyph","sending":"while the send is in flight, the same slot shows chat.thread.sending in color.global.textSecondary","action":"tap the line or the message → send again (menu item chat.thread.menu.retry does the same)","haptic":"haptic.global.error when a send fails"}` | Retro + Apple Messages | own message whose send failed / is in flight | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt (message_thread_delivery_status_sending 'Sending', message_thread_context_menu_retry 'Retry', media_viewer_comment_send_failed_title "Couldn't send comment"); references/auto/imessage/apple-support/messages-not-delivered.png (red label under the failed message, tap to retry); design/components.md C-10 (red text) | SOURCED | Retro's states and words, Messages' placement (label under the message), drawn as Retro red text (C-10) · Messages' red '!' circle is not drawn (another app's visual). ## `chat.voice`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `chat.voice.layout.capsule` | `{"component":"C-06 fill capsule with the anatomy of C-07: leading glyph, then the label","height":"size.global.pillHeight","corners":"radius.global.capsule","fill":"page: color.global.control (iOS) / color.global.surfaceSecondary.android; on media (photo.viewer): color.global.controlOnDeep","glyph":"icon.global.play (playing: icon.global.pause) at size.global.iconSmall, color.global.textPrimary","label":"duration per chat.voice.layout.durationFormat, text role pill, color.global.textPrimary","padding":"size.global.pillPaddingLeading → glyph → size.global.pillIconGap → label → size.global.pillPaddingTrailing","width":"hugs its content (no width per duration)"}` | Retro | voice message in the thread, and a photo's voice note in the viewer | design/components.md C-06 (fill capsule), C-07 (outline CTA anatomy, H43), C-25 (video duration pill '1:00', PAY) | SOURCED | composition of Retro components C-06 / C-07 / C-25 in WhatsApp's play-and-duration order · No waveform: Retro has no waveform component and another app's bars would be another app's visual (MERGE RS-7). Closes the BLOCKED width rule (the capsule hugs its label). |
| `chat.voice.layout.durationFormat` | `{"pattern":"m:ss","example":"0:38","digits":"monospaced"}` | Retro | duration label of a voice message | references/auto/retro/lazyweb/retro-lazyweb-20260529-paywall.png (video duration pill '1:00', monospaced digits); design/components.md C-25 | SOURCED | Retro's media-duration format · format · Replaces Yope's 'mm:ss' ('00:38'). |
| `chat.voice.layout.playback` | `{"tap":"the capsule toggles play / pause; one voice message plays at a time (starting another stops the first)","label":"total duration when idle; elapsed time while playing or paused mid-way","end":"back to the idle state at the end"}` | Telegram + WhatsApp | voice playback behaviour | references/auto/telegram/ (blog 'Flexible Playback Speed': elapsed time while playing, Gap fill G-8); references/auto/whatsapp/help/faq-1165411581043811-play-voice-iphone.png (play / pause) | SOURCED | documented behaviour of two references |
| `chat.voice.layout.recording` | `{"start":"press and hold the composer's mic glyph: recording runs while the finger is down","send":"lift the finger → the voice message is sent","cancel":"slide left → recording discarded","lock":"slide up onto the lock circle above the mic → keeps recording hands-free; then the send glyph sends, the trash glyph discards","field":"while recording the field shows: icon.global.mic in color.global.destructive · elapsed time (chat.voice.layout.durationFormat, text role body, color.global.textPrimary) · icon.global.back + chat.voice.recording.slideToCancel in color.global.textSecondary","lockCircle":"C-11 circle size.global.circleButton, color.global.control, icon.global.lock in color.global.textPrimary, centred over the mic glyph, size.global.pillGap above the field","haptic":"none on start / stop (HIG: do not disturb the microphone); haptic.global.send when sent"}` | WhatsApp + Telegram | recording a voice message | references/auto/whatsapp/help/faq-657157755756612-send-voice-iphone.png ('Press and hold [mic] next to the text field…', '…slide [‹] to cancel…', hands-free); references/auto/whatsapp/store/whatsapp-store-02.png (recording row); references/auto/telegram/store/telegram-store-03.png (recording row with lock) | SOURCED | documented gestures (Gap fill G-9); drawn with Retro components (C-11, C-13, C-10 red glyph) |

## `collage.share`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `collage.share.layout.export` | `{"aspect":"9:16","widthPx":1080,"heightPx":1920,"source":"collages.share_path page n (worker share-<n>.jpg)","watermark":"none"}` | Meta developer docs (standards: accessibility thresholds, share-image specs) | the image handed to the share sheet | https://developers.facebook.com/docs/instagram-platform/sharing-to-stories/ (2026-06-30): 'Minimum dimensions 720x1280. Recommended image ratios 9:16 or 9:18'; design/research/yope-b.md §8 C7 (Yope exports are 9:16) | VERIFIED | 1080 × 1920 = the 9:16 frame at 1080 px wide (computed, ≥ the stated minimum) · Yope's export watermark is its wordmark (do-not-copy); a {app_name} watermark needs the owner (yope-b proposal layout.share.export). |

| `haptic.global.error` | `{"ios":"notification.error","android":"REJECT"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a task failed | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.longPressStart` | `{"ios":"system (context menu plays its own haptic; no custom generator)","android":"LONG_PRESS"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a long press starts (context menu) | HIG Menus / context menus; Android HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform behaviour |
| `haptic.global.reaction` | `{"ios":"system (context menu plays its own haptic; no custom generator)","android":"LONG_PRESS"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | the long press that opens the reaction picker on a photo | as haptic.global.longPressStart (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform behaviour |
| `haptic.global.select` | `{"ios":"selection","android":"SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK"}` | Retro | tick: one per step on a dial / scrubber (Rewind dial, friend picker, video trim), a segmented choice, a toggled selection | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt 'Tick haptic': SDK ≥ 34 performHapticFeedback(27 SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK) else 4 CLOCK_TICK (lh2, e25, f9e, hw, ig, kbh, p6a, pyj); Rewind tab w9e collects the dial flow into the tick helper — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; iOS: HIG Playing haptics 'Selection' (UISelectionFeedbackGenerator) | PLATFORM | call sites in the package; iOS platform equivalent · Haptics.kt maps SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK to CLOCK_TICK below API 34, exactly as Retro does. |
| `haptic.global.send` | `{"ios":"notification.success","android":"CONFIRM"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | confirm / send: a photo or message was sent | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.shutter` | `{"ios":"none","android":"none"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | shutter press (photo / video capture) | HIG Playing haptics: 'Ensure that haptic vibrations don't disrupt experiences involving … the camera' (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform rule |
| `haptic.global.success` | `{"ios":"notification.success","android":"CONFIRM"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a task completed successfully | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.tap` | `{"ios":"none","android":"VIRTUAL_KEY"}` | Retro | tap on a button, tile or card (played just before the action) | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt 'Tap haptic wrapper': performHapticFeedback(1 VIRTUAL_KEY) then action (cm9: 17 wrapped click handlers; gd, h21, kbh, w1a, wc1, xk9, n9e) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8 | PLATFORM | call sites in the package; iOS standard buttons play no haptic (Retro's iOS app was not inspected) |
| `haptic.global.warning` | `{"ios":"notification.warning","android":"CONFIRM"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a destructive action was confirmed | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |

## `invite.appclip`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `invite.appclip.layout.association` | `{"aasaAppClips":"appclips.apps = [TEAM_ID.BUNDLE_ID.Clip]","aasaAppLinks":"applinks.details[].components = [{\"/\": \"/i/*\"}] for the full app","invocation":"Safari App Clip card from the invite page's apple-itunes-app meta tag (app-clip-display=card); Messages link previews; QR codes of the link","defaultExperience":"App Store Connect default App Clip experience + an advanced experience for https://<domain>/i/ (prefix match)"}` | Apple HIG | how the App Clip is associated with and invoked from invite links | https://developer.apple.com/documentation/app_clips/associating-your-app-clip-with-your-website ; https://developer.apple.com/documentation/app_clips/supporting-invocations-from-your-website-and-the-messages-app ; Retro links its /u/ page to an App Clip card (references/auto/retro/web/retro.app-web-copy-excerpts.txt apple-itunes-app meta, design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §7) | VERIFIED |  |
| `invite.appclip.layout.cardLimits` | `{"headerImage":"1800×1200 px, no transparency, no text","titleMaxCharacters":30,"subtitleMaxCharacters":56,"actions":"Open \| View \| Play (one of the preset verbs)"}` | Apple HIG | App Clip card asset and text limits | https://developer.apple.com/help/app-store-connect/offer-app-clip-experiences/configure-the-default-app-clip-experience/ ; https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/app-clips | PLATFORM |  |
| `invite.appclip.layout.fullAppOverlay` | `{"position":"bottom","when":"after join_group succeeds (a natural pause: the 'Welcome to {group_name}!' state), never before","api":"SKOverlay(configuration: SKOverlay.AppClipConfiguration(position: .bottom))","repeat":"once per launch of the clip"}` | Apple HIG | prompt to get the full app from the App Clip | https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/app-clips ('Recommending your app to App Clip users': SKOverlay after the task, at a natural pause) | PLATFORM |  |
| `invite.appclip.layout.messagesCard` | `{"header":"the App Clip card image (App Store Connect header image) — no text in the image","title":"App Store Connect card title (≤ 30 characters)","subtitle":"copy invite.appclip.card.subtitle (≤ 56 characters)","action":"copy invite.appclip.card.action (one of Apple's preset verbs)","display":"app-clip-display=card on the invite page (Retro uses the card display, not the banner)"}` | Apple HIG | App Clip card shown by Safari / Messages for an invite link | https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/app-clips ; references/auto/retro/web/retro.app-web-copy-excerpts.txt (Retro: app-clip-display=card) | PLATFORM |  |

## `notifications.push`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `notifications.push.layout.channels` | `{"daily_invitation":"IMPORTANCE_HIGH","new_post":"IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT","reactions_replies":"IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT","recap":"IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT","birthday_request":"IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT","tribute":"IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT"}` | Material 3 | Android notification channels that carry the push texts (one per kind of event) | android/core/push/src/main/kotlin/app/push/Notifications.kt (AppChannel); https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/views/notifications/channels (names ≤ 40, descriptions ≤ 300 characters; importance immutable); design/research/locket.md §9 (Locket's channels, one per kind) | PLATFORM | existing channel ids; platform rule · AppChannel.YearStory has no event the worker sends (backend/worker/COPY_KEYS.md) → no channel copy; owner decision whether to register it. |
| `notifications.push.layout.ios` | `{"interruptionLevel":"active (passive during quiet hours)","threadId":"group id","newPostAttachment":"widget rendition attached by the notification service extension","sound":"default"}` | Apple HIG | iOS alert presentation for every push text | docs/PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS.md §1.2, §1.6; https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/notifications; design/copy-patterns.md tenet filters (no TIME SENSITIVE) | PLATFORM | native API + rule |

| `onboarding.permission.notifications.layout.preAlert` | `{"structure":"onboarding page (C-33 variant): display title (text role displayMedium) + explanation (text role body, color.global.textSecondary) leading-aligned at size.global.screenMargin, primary capsule C-05 pinned to the bottom","ios":"one capsule ('Enable notifications') that triggers UNUserNotificationCenter.requestAuthorization; no close, skip or back (HIG pre-alert rule)","android":"API 33+: the same page plus a text button 'Maybe later' (C-08 secondary link) under the capsule; then the POST_NOTIFICATIONS runtime dialog; API ≤ 32: page skipped (no runtime permission)"}` | Apple HIG | notifications pre-alert page | https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/privacy#Pre-alert-screens-windows-or-views ; https://developer.android.com/training/permissions/requesting ('Always provide the option to cancel an educational UI flow'); Retro page: references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt onboarding_notification_permission_retry_title / _subtitle (onboarding step NOTIFICATION_PERMISSION_RETRY, design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §6.3) | VERIFIED | Retro shows this as an onboarding page in its sign-up order; the page's image was not captured (RV §2 rows 36–41). |
| copy `onboarding.permission.notifications.continue` | “Enable notifications” | Retro | verbatim | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt `activity_enable_push_upsell_cta` | SOURCED | retro.voice.button |

| copy `onboarding.permission.notifications.skip` | “Maybe later” | Retro | verbatim | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt `media_viewer_suggested_tag_maybe_later` | SOURCED | retro.voice.button · Android only (Android requires a way out of educational UI); iOS shows no skip (HIG pre-alert rule). |

## `onthisday`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `color.onthisday.dial.tick` | `#FFFFFF4D` | Retro | dial ticks (all slots except the indicator) | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): 26 ticks at radius 200 px, peak vs the background half a pitch either side, e.g. (157,147,138) over (114,100,87), (134,128,120) over (83,73,62); references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide) (JPEG): 7 ticks, alpha 0.30-0.33 | SOURCED | alpha of white per channel = (peak − bg) ÷ (255 − bg); RWA median 0.300 (all channels 0.295-0.307 on the ticks away from the share circle) = 0x4D · ±0.01 alpha (RWA PNG); ±0.02 (RWB JPEG) · Same value as color.global.textTertiary dark and APK white @ 0.3 (Retro's alpha table, RP §3.3); kept as its own token because its role is the dial on media in both appearances. |
| `color.onthisday.subtitle` | `#FFFFFFC2` | Retro | subtitle under the date title ('On this day · {name}' / 'Memories · {name}'), over the photo | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-panels.png (press render, Dec 2025) phone 1 'On this week' (x455-615, y450-490 px) and references/auto/retro/store/retro-store-05.png (App Store render, 2.389 px/pt) (x205-435, y850-895 px): glyph core (218,204,197) over the wall behind it (38-39,65-66,52) | SOURCED | alpha of white solved from the brightest 3 % of glyph pixels against the 30th-percentile background in the same box: R 0.83, G 0.73, B 0.71 → mean 0.76 = 0xC2 · ±0.06 alpha (the three channels disagree: the text may carry vibrancy over the photo) · Retro draws the subtitle white at reduced opacity (components C-35 called it not measurable); this is the closest solve. Over the photo its contrast is photo-dependent (no top scrim in any capture). |
| `count.onthisday.dial.ticks` | `44` | Retro | number of tick slots around the dial (long and short alternate) | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide) centre (660, 2288.5) and references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide) centre (630, 2217.5): tick angles at a constant pitch 8.19° (25 + 26 consecutive ticks, ±0.07°) | SOURCED | background-subtracted polar profile (2880 angles, radius 100-300 px); 360° ÷ 8.19° = 43.96 · exact (the pitch rules out 43 and 45) · design/research/retro-timehop.md counted '≈45 (44/45)' by crossings; the pitch settles it at 44. |
| `dimension.onthisday.controls.centerY` | `139.2 pt` | Retro | dial centre (and the centres of the share / random circles) above the safe-area bottom | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): dial centre y=2217.5 px = 739.2 pt on a 912-pt screen; safe-area bottom 878 pt (34-pt home-indicator inset); share circle y-centre 2217 (x107-299); references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): centre 762.8 pt on a 956-pt screen → 159.2 pt above its safe-area bottom | SOURCED | (912 − 34) − 739.2 · ±0.5 pt (RWB); RWA differs by 20 pt (another dial state, MERGE RM-3) · In Retro the tab bar sits between the dial and the home indicator; our page has no tab bar, and the controls keep Retro's distance from the bottom so the photo is framed the same way. |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.diameter` | `150 pt` | Retro | Rewind dial: outer diameter of the tick ring | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): centre (630, 2217.5); tick outer radius 225 px (p50 over 720 angles, design/research/retro-timehop.md) / 226.5 px (this pass, edge at 25 levels above the local background over 2880 angles) | SOURCED | 2 × outer radius ÷ 3 px/pt · ±2 pt · RWA (same month, larger phone) draws the ring smaller: outer radius 214.5 px = 143 pt, with a longer indicator (28 pt) and a larger play glyph — read as another state of the dial (it was not captured at rest); RWB's resting dial is used (MERGE RM-3). The store render ST-05 shows an earlier dial (≈133 pt, 9.8° pitch) — superseded by the RAW captures. |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.glyph` | `35 pt` | Retro | play / pause glyph in the dial centre: ink height | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): play triangle ink x589-683, y2165-2269 (31.7 × 35.0 pt); references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): x616-716, y2233-2344 (33.7 × 37.3 pt) | SOURCED | bounding box of pixels > 245 ÷ 3; RWB (dial at rest) used · ±0.5 pt · Glyphs icon.global.play / icon.global.pause (fill variant = a state, icon.global.variantRule), colour color.global.textOnMedia; size the symbol so its ink is this tall. |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.indicatorLength` | `16.7 pt` | Retro | indicator (the current memory's slot): solid tick from the ring's outer edge inward | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): indicator at 178° (9 o'clock), row y=2224 white (255) from x404 to x453.5 → radius 176.5-226 px; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): indicator at 6° from radius 130 to 214 px (28 pt) | SOURCED | ink length ÷ 3 (RWB, dial at rest) · ±0.5 pt · Colour = color.global.textOnMedia (both captures read 255). RWA's 28-pt indicator goes with its smaller ring (another state, see dimension.onthisday.dial.diameter). Capture that would settle the states: 10 s screen recording of the Rewind dial dragged and released. |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.tickLong` | `19.8 pt` | Retro | long tick (even slots) length, measured from the ring's outer edge inward | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide) column x=638 across the 6 o'clock tick: ink y2384-2443 (partial pixels at both ends); polar profile inner radius 167 px vs outer 226.5 px; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide) 155.5 → 214.5 px | SOURCED | ink length incl. partial-pixel coverage ÷ 3; RWA and RWB agree (59-59.5 px) · ±0.5 pt · design/research/retro-timehop.md gives 18 from the 50th-percentile inner radius over all angles (171 px); this pass measured the ticks themselves. |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.tickShort` | `11.8 pt` | Retro | short tick (odd slots) length, from the ring's outer edge inward | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide) polar profile: short ticks inner radius 191 px, outer 226.5 px; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): 179.5 → 214.5 px | SOURCED | (outer − inner) ÷ 3; both captures 35-35.5 px · ±0.5 pt |
| `dimension.onthisday.dial.tickWidth` | `3.2 pt` | Retro | width (stroke) of every tick and of the indicator | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide) row y=2488 across the 6 o'clock tick: x663-672 full (129) + partial 96 / 107 over background 75 (≈9.9 px); indicator at 6°: 9.5 px across; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide) row y=2415: 8 full + 2 partial (≈9 px), indicator rows 2220-2229 (10 px) | SOURCED | full pixels + partial-pixel coverage ÷ 3 · ±0.3 pt · Ends look square at this scale (the long-tick column starts with one partial pixel); a round cap of 1.6 pt cannot be ruled out. |

| `duration.onthisday.memoryChange` | `700 ms` | VidDay | cross-fade from one memory to the next while playing (automatic advance) | https://static.vidday.com/vidday_theme_samples/theme_happy_birthday.mp4 (listed on https://www.vidday.com/examples; 1920x1080, 30 fps, 135.72 s); per-frame diff references/auto/vidday/frames/vidday-theme-happy-birthday-framediff.csv: photo-to-photo crossfades 23.233-23.933, 26.467-27.167, 30.467-31.167, 64.133-64.833, 68.167-68.867, 80.500-81.200, 92.133-92.833 s (21-22 changed frames each) | SOURCED | first to last changed frame of each crossfade (mean abs grey diff > 0.8 on a 192-px decode), 30 fps · ±33 ms (1 frame); curve not measurable (duration only) · Mechanics (memory montage, VidDay; MERGE G2-17): Retro's own photo change was not captured (Fast Company: photos 'flip by', text only). A step made by dragging the dial cross-fades over duration.global.crossfade (100 ms, Retro) instead, so scrubbing keeps up with the finger. Reduce Motion: the cross-fade stays (it is already the substitute, layout.global.reduceMotion). |

Playing stops after the last memory (the dial shows play again). |
| `layout.onthisday.controls` | `{"order":"share circle, dial, random circle","arrangement":"equal gaps across the screen width: edge \| share \| dial \| random \| edge (the dial's box = dimension.onthisday.dial.diameter)","circles":"size.global.circleButtonLarge, fill color.global.glass, glyphs icon.global.share / icon.global.random in a size.global.icon box, color.global.textOnMedia","paused":"share, dial (icon.global.play) and random shown","playing":"share and random hidden (fade duration.global.fade, easing.global.standard); the dial shows icon.global.pause","scrim":"layout.global.photoScrim behind the controls"}` | Retro | bottom control row of the Rewind page | references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-b.jpg (RAW iOS 1260x2736, 3.000 px/pt, 420 pt wide): share x107-299, dial ticks 403.5-856.5, random x959-1151 on 1260 px → gaps 35.7 / 34.8 / 34.8 / 35.7 pt; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-device-a.png (RAW iOS 1320x2868, 3.000 px/pt, 440 pt wide): share x118.5-310.5, random x1009.5-1201.5 on 1320 px → edge gaps 39.5 pt, circle → 150-pt dial box 41 pt; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-panels.png (press render, Dec 2025) phone 1 and references/auto/retro/store/retro-store-05.png (App Store render, 2.389 px/pt): playing (pause glyph) with no share / random circles; references/auto/retro/press/tc-2025-12-rewind-panels.png (press render, Dec 2025) phone 2: paused (play glyph) with both circles | SOURCED | edge runs on the circle row (luminance step ≥ 6); equal-gap model fits both RAW captures within 1.5 pt · ±1.5 pt · Share / random glyph ink measures 19.7 × 24.7 and 23.7 × 23.3 pt on RWB, inside the 24-pt glyph box. |

## `safety.report`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `safety.report.layout.flow` | `{"entry":"menu item 'Report Post' / 'Report Message' / 'Report Profile' / 'Report Group' (C-29 native menu)","reasons":"native action sheet (iOS confirmationDialog with a visible title; Android ModalBottomSheet list) titled with Retro's question; one row per reason (safety.report.layout.reasons), Cancel last","submit":"choosing a reason files report(kind, id, reason) at once — no second screen; confirmation 'Submitted!' (C-26)","dontLike":"'I don't like it' files nothing: it opens a second action sheet 'Next Steps' with 'Leave the group' and 'Block {first_name}' (safety.block)"}` | Retro | report flow | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt media_viewer_report_reason_title 'Why are you reporting this post?', media_viewer_report_reason_* ('I don't like it', 'Nudity or sexual activity', 'Other'), media_viewer_report_dont_like_title 'Next Steps' + _leave_journal / _send_message / _unfriend, profile_report_sheet_title, profile_report_reason_*, profile_report_submit_success 'Submitted!'; design/research/retro-visual.md §4 #20 (native menus and sheets) | SOURCED | Retro's report strings (reason list + next-steps branch) presented in native action sheets (C-29) · Retro's sheets were not captured as images; the native action sheet is the platform component for a list of choices (HIG Action sheets). Retro's 'Send a message' and 'Unfriend' next steps map to this app's 'Block' (no direct messages, no friendships) — MERGE RA-12. |
| `safety.report.layout.reasons` | `{"post":"I don't like it · Nudity or sexual activity · Other","message":"I don't like it · Nudity or sexual activity · Other","user":"It's impersonating someone · It's inappropriate · Other","group":"It's impersonating someone · It's inappropriate · Other","sent":"the reason's English text is sent as p_reason (docs/API.md report)"}` | Retro | report reasons per target kind | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-strings.txt media_viewer_report_reason_dont_like_it, _nudity, _other (posts); profile_report_reason_impersonation, _inappropriate, _other (profiles) | SOURCED | Retro's two reason lists; messages take the post list, groups take the profile list (noun swap in the title only) · Was BLOCKED (moderation categories, owner). Retro's lists close it as a UI list; the owner may still extend the categories for moderation (docs/API.md §12 item 19). |

## `settings.account`

| `settings.root.layout.list` | `{"component":"design/components.md C-14 on both platforms (custom rows; not List(.insetGrouped) / M3 ListItem)","sheet":"page sheet (C-29) with its own navigation: header = centred title (text role sheetTitle) + trailing close circle (C-11 circle on the page); sub-pages push inside the sheet with the native back button and the same centred title","page":"color.global.background (iOS) / color.global.background.android","sectionGap":"size.global.sectionGap between a group's bottom and the next section header"}` | Retro | settings sheet structure | references/auto/retro/video/ig-DJXvmqRv2_N-referrals-sheet-f042-059.png frames 42–44 (Settings sheet: 'Settings' centred + '×' circle, profile card, small grey section headers, Fill rows with chevrons, Widgets carousel); design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §5.1 (sheet spring, V1) | SOURCED | Retro's Settings sheet (VID-SET) with the component metrics of C-14 · Section gap not measurable in the video frames; size.global.sectionGap (36.4) is Retro's section rhythm on the profile (composition). |

| `week.history.layout.currentWeek` | `{"empty":"no photos yet: the add tile first, then one empty-day tile per day Monday → Sunday (dated: month / day / weekday)","withPhotos":"photo tiles in time order, then the add tile, then one placeholder tile (blank fill) per remaining day without a photo","addTile":"C-17 add tile → camera.capture with this group (walls.mine: any group)","readOnly":"where the viewer cannot post (walls.member) the add tile is left out"}` | Retro | the current week's strip (empty-day, add and placeholder tiles) | references/auto/retro/lazyweb/retro-lazyweb-20251020-week43-dark.png, references/auto/retro/lazyweb/retro-lazyweb-20260528-week22-dark-a.png (empty week: '+' first, then dated tiles for every day incl. later days); references/auto/retro/press/engadget-2026-08-profile.jpg (2026-08 live app: 'Mon', 'Mon', add tile, then blank grey tiles); references/auto/retro/store/retro-store-02.png (render: photos then the add tile) | SOURCED | RAW and real-device captures (2025–2026); the render's missing tiles after '+' lose to the live capture · rule · How many blank tiles follow the add tile is not legible in ENG-P (the strip runs off-screen): one per remaining day is the development rule (Gap). |

## `widget.android`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

This is the headline's curve (duration.year.story.reveal.headline 800 ms, dimension .headlineShift). The subline follows a different, decelerating curve: easing.year.story.reveal.subline (new token). No single curve fits both (best joint fit rms 0.12 = 5-7 px). The earlier 'not fitted' note used only the three summary points (layout.year.story.reveal.samples). Documented curves tried: FastOutSlowIn (0.4,0,0.2,1) rms 0.060, easeInOut 0.112. Mechanics kept from Spotify Wrapped (year-story pacing; docs/REDESIGN_BRIEF.md): applies to the headline / subline of text cards inside the frame. Reduce Motion: cross-fade of the same durations (layout.global.reduceMotion). |
